Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in supersymmetry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 32 and the invariant I(A,B) = 758 mod 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by supersymmetry generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 32, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 758. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any supersymmetry module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 183. The projective resolution has length at most 106 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 183 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the supersymmetry structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 106. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
